TimelineMonthsObjects中文

Codex

Products/Tools · OpenAI

CLI, cloud, IDE and desktop workbench product lines starting in 2025; do not consider 2021 namesake models as cost products.

The earliest recorded event is not necessarily the first release. This page reflects the materials found within the current research scope.

Also known as: Codex CLI, Codex app, Codex

Official updates & historical sources

1037 recorded official updates · Through 2026-09-10

Standalone update log

Differentiate between CLI, app, web, and model; save old Help Center logs

Additional official source

Distinguish products, SDKs, specifications and release channels. A single update should not be counted as multiple events.

Coverage notes

Only dated public updates are indexed. Internal builds, nightly/canary builds, undated records and unread historical entries are excluded; this is not a complete release history.

Version records retain their dates and original sources. Topic labels are an index, not claims of new capabilities or independently verified results.

Codex → Codex special model (2025-)

Product access coding special model

05-16

Codex cloud research preview: Based on codex-1 (o3 optimized for software engineering), each task has an independent cloud sandbox, which can repair, write functions, and submit PRs in parallel. People shift from driving each step to delegating tasks and reviewing results.

public node · See original source text for status and restrictions.

Original source 1

09-15

GPT-5-Codex is released, optimized for agentic coding in terminals, IDEs, clouds, and GitHub; context compression and long sessions are incorporated into the complete running experience.

public node · See original source text for status and restrictions.

Original source 1

12-18

GPT-5.2-Codex is released, continuing to strengthen compressed long tasks, migration/refactoring, Windows and network security; the first day is the Codex surfaces of paid ChatGPT, and the API opening is still being arranged later.

public node · See original source text for status and restrictions.

Original source 1

Codex → ChatGPT Work

Work reuses Codex’s execution technology

07-09

GPT-5.6 Sol/Terra/Luna was fully released, and ChatGPT Work was launched on the same day, using Codex technology for long work tasks across applications and files.

public node · The model is stratified by capability/cost and combined with the general work Agent; the previous limited preview is different from this GA, and the specific packages and platforms are still open in stages.

Original source 1 Original source 2

OpenClaw → Codex

Reuse Codex app-server execution

05-14

OpenClaw switches OpenAI model execution to the Codex app-server by default: Codex is responsible for inner loops such as model rounds, tool continuation, and compression, while OpenClaw retains outer layers such as channels, persona, memory, and cron.

public node · It is a demonstration of "interoperable runtime": universal personal Agents can be combined with Coding Agent Harness, and product relationships cannot be explained only by interface appearance.

Original source 1

Codex → Agent Skills

Workbench support Skills

02-02

Codex macOS app is released, adding multi-tasking parallelism, isolated worktree, Skills and Automations.

public node · Not just another chat shell: start bringing long-running tasks, parallel execution, and repetitive work management to your desktop workbench. Windows is a follow-up update to 03-04.

Original source 1

2025-04

Direct event

04-16

o3 and o4-mini were released on the same day as the open source Codex CLI. CLI reads, modifies, and executes code in the local terminal; this is the first CLI of the 2025 Codex product line and must be distinguished from the 2021 Codex model and the May cloud Codex.

public node · See original source text for status and restrictions.

Original source 1

Human–agent collaboration that month (industry-wide)

People are responsible for business discovery, architecture, and handover. Web prototypes cannot replace the establishment of requirements.

Read and write warehouses and execute codes independently; cross-agent communication standards are proposed, and non-coding delivery is checked according to tasks

Full industry context for this month

2025-05

Direct event

05-16

Codex cloud research preview: Based on codex-1 (o3 optimized for software engineering), each task has an independent cloud sandbox, which can repair, write functions, and submit PRs in parallel. People shift from driving each step to delegating tasks and reviewing results.

public node · See original source text for status and restrictions.

Original source 1

Human–agent collaboration that month (industry-wide)

Assign tasks, review results, and connect prototypes with existing systems

Modify, test, and commit changes autonomously; AlphaEvolve generates and evaluates code to discover algorithms

Full industry context for this month

2025-06

Direct event

06-03

Cloud Codex extends to ChatGPT Plus and allows users to enable internet access for execution. Environment preparation/dependency acquisition friction is reduced while the network becomes a single control plane.

public node · See original source text for status and restrictions.

Human–agent collaboration that month (industry-wide)

There is still an installation threshold for human management tasks; too much parallelism can easily cause the context to be lost.

Independently load professional methods on demand and work in parallel; think about non-coding products and expand professional tasks

Full industry context for this month

2025-11

Related context

11-19

GPT-5.1-Codex-Max is released, targeting long-range tasks across context windows and higher token efficiency. Compression/continuation becomes a joint optimization object of models and tools and cannot be written in infinite context.

public node · See original source text for status and restrictions.

Human–agent collaboration that month (industry-wide)

People delegate document work and larger functions, still coordinate interfaces, materials, acceptance

Independently read and write office materials, call tools, and dispatch multiple agents; scale does not equal reliability

Full industry context for this month

2026-02

Direct event

02-02

Codex macOS app is released, adding multi-tasking parallelism, isolated worktree, Skills and Automations.

public node · Not just another chat shell: start bringing long-running tasks, parallel execution, and repetitive work management to your desktop workbench. Windows is a follow-up update to 03-04.

Original source 1

Related context

02-05；02-12

GPT-5.3-Codex and Claude Opus 4.6 were released on the same day; the former enhances long tasks and mid-execution steering, and the latter brings Claude Code agent teams research preview and API compaction; 02-12 Codex-Spark provides a low-latency route.

public node · The competition already covers "model + runtime interaction". Claude agent teams and 1M context all have preview boundaries in the initial stage; Spark is not immediately available to all API users at launch.

Human–agent collaboration that month (industry-wide)

People are assigned to work, turn around, and manage the budget; dependent tasks cannot be arbitrarily parallel

Autonomous multi-task execution, context continuity, repetitive task automation; office capability expansion

Full industry context for this month

2026-03

Direct event

03-05

GPT-5.4 simultaneously enters ChatGPT, API and Codex, integrates the previous Codex programming capabilities into a common model, and provides native computer use, tool search and up to 1M context.

public node · Programming, browser/application operations, and office delivery begin to share a single model layer. Millions of contexts do not automatically equate to cross-session long-term memory.

Human–agent collaboration that month (industry-wide)

People explain goals, authorization tools, and evaluation results; novices still differentiate between model products

Full industry context for this month

2026-04

Direct event

04-16

GPT-Rosalind opens research previews to qualified customers, supporting evidence collation, hypothesis formation and experimental planning; the Codex Life Sciences Research plugin connects scientific tools and data sources.

public node · Coding Agent’s files, code, and tool execution infrastructure become the entrance to scientific research. It is not available to any user, and the experimental plan generation cannot be written as a wet experiment has been completed.

2026-05

Direct event

05-14

OpenClaw switches OpenAI model execution to the Codex app-server by default: Codex is responsible for inner loops such as model rounds, tool continuation, and compression, while OpenClaw retains outer layers such as channels, persona, memory, and cron.

public node · It is a demonstration of "interoperable runtime": universal personal Agents can be combined with Coding Agent Harness, and product relationships cannot be explained only by interface appearance.

Original source 1

Direct event

05-14

Codex is connected to the ChatGPT mobile app, and users can remotely view and redirect tasks running on laptops, development machines or remote environments.

public node · The control interface of long-term tasks is separated from the actual execution location; being manageable by a mobile phone does not mean that the mobile phone executes all codes locally.

Human–agent collaboration that month (industry-wide)

People define work, review method artifacts, and handle out-of-date information and expenses

Autonomous discovery/dispatch/execution/validation loop; cross-application work, experience extraction and experimentation loop

Full industry context for this month

2026-07

Related context

07-09

GPT-5.6 Sol/Terra/Luna was fully released, and ChatGPT Work was launched on the same day, using Codex technology for long work tasks across applications and files.

public node · The model is stratified by capability/cost and combined with the general work Agent; the previous limited preview is different from this GA, and the specific packages and platforms are still open in stages.

Human–agent collaboration that month (industry-wide)

Humans use desktop and enterprise context to delegate tasks and still accept, recover, and operate

Autonomous dismantling of work, calling tools, and continuous execution; limited preview and exploration of experimental equipment operations

Full industry context for this month

2026-09

Direct event

09-03 Begin phased rollout

GPT-6 Astra is released, combining stronger computer/browser operation, programming, professional work and scientific research; Codex Harness simultaneously improves computer operation processes.

public node · The new model release is not just about calculating problem scores, but also involves asynchronous clarification, maintaining task goals, cross-application execution and permission judgment. Network capabilities are officially classified as Critical; publicity announcements should not be equated with universal AGI verification.
